                [32m'## Background\n'[39m +
                [32m'You are building a stateful stream-processing pipeline with **Bytewax** (a Python-native stream processor) that ingests aligned multi-sensor time-series readings, groups them into fixed event-time windows, and computes the pairwise **Pearson correlation** matrix between sensors within each window. The per-window correlations are written to a local JSONL sink.\n'[39m +
                [32m'\n'[39m +
                [32m'## Requirements\n'[39m +
                [32m'- Read sensor readings from a local JSONL input file.\n'[39m +
                [32m'- Group readings into fixed-length, non-overlapping **event-time** windows.\n'[39m +
                [32m'- Within each window, for every pair of sensors, compute the Pearson correlation coefficient using only the timestamps at which BOTH sensors of the pair reported a value in that window (sample alignment by timestamp).\n'[39m +
                [32m'- Apply a minimum-overlap threshold, deterministic pair ordering, and stable rounding as specified below.\n'[39m +
                [32m'- Emit the per-window correlation results to a local JSONL sink.\n'[39m +
                [32m'\n'[39m +
                [32m'## Implementation Hints\n'[39m +
                [32m'- Project path: `/home/user/project`.\n'[39m +
                [32m'- Use `bytewax==0.21.1`. The dataflow must be defined at module path `dataflow` (i.e. `/home/user/project/dataflow.py`) as a top-level attribute named `flow`, and must run to completion (exit code 0) with:\n'[39m +
                [32m'  `python -m bytewax.run dataflow:flow` executed with the working directory set to the project path.\n'[39m +
                [32m'- Input file: `/home/user/project/data/readings.jsonl`. Each line is a JSON object with exactly the keys `sensor` (string), `ts` (ISO-8601 UTC timestamp string ending in `Z`, e.g. `2024-01-01T00:00:00Z`), and `value` (number). Readings appear in nondecreasing `ts` order, and each `(sensor, ts)` combination occurs at most once.\n'[39m +
                [32m'- Windowing: fixed-length **tumbling** event-time windows of exactly 60 seconds, aligned so that a window boundary falls on the epoch `2024-01-01T00:00:00Z`. Window open times are inclusive and close times are exclusive; a reading with timestamp `t` belongs to the single window `[align + k*60s, align + (k+1)*60s)` that contains it.\n'[39m +
                [32m"- Overlap definition: within one window, two sensors overlap at a timestamp only when BOTH reported a value at that exact timestamp. A pair's correlation is computed over its overlapping timestamps only, pairing each sensor's value at each shared timestamp.\n"[39m +
                [32m'- Pearson correlation over the overlapping paired samples `(x_i, y_i)` with means `x_bar`, `y_bar`:\n'[39m +
                [32m'  `r = sum_i (x_i - x_bar)*(y_i - y_bar) / sqrt( sum_i (x_i - x_bar)^2 * sum_i (y_i - y_bar)^2 )`\n'[39m +
                [32m'- Minimum-overlap threshold: include a sensor pair only when its number of overlapping timestamps `n` satisfies `n >= 3`. Pairs with `n < 3` must be omitted entirely.\n'[39m +
                [32m'- Undefined correlation: when `n >= 3` but the denominator above is 0 (i.e. at least one of the two sensors is constant across the overlap), represent `r` as JSON `null`.\n'[39m +
                [32m'- Rounding: round every finite `r` to 6 decimal places.\n'[39m +
                [32m'- Output file: `/home/user/project/output/correlations.jsonl`. Write exactly one JSON object per window that has at least one qualifying pair (`n >= 3`); windows with no qualifying pair must not produce a line. Output lines must be ordered by window start time ascending.\n'[39m +
                [32m'- Each output object must contain exactly these keys:\n'[39m +
                [32m'  - `window_start`: the window open time formatted as `%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ` (UTC).\n'[39m +
                [32m'  - `window_end`: the window close time (open + 60s) formatted as `%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ` (UTC).\n'[39m +
                [32m'  - `correlations`: a JSON array of objects, each with exactly the keys `pair` (a 2-element array `[sensor_a, sensor_b]` with `sensor_a < sensor_b` by lexicographic string comparison), `n` (integer count of overlapping timestamps), and `r` (the rounded correlation number, or `null`). The array must be ordered ascending by `pair`, i.e. by `sensor_a` then `sensor_b`.'[39m

                    [32m'    """Abstract class to define a sense of time for windowing.\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m"    If you're just getting started with windowing operators, see the\n"[39m +
                    [32m'    concrete subclasses of {py:obj}`Clock` for built-in options.\n'[39m +

                    [32m'\n'[39m +

                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        Called on each new upstream item.\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +

                    [32m'        relevant state must be retained within.\n'[39m +

                    [32m'    def on_eof(self) -> datetime:\n'[39m +

                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        This is used to determine when the runtime should wake up to\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        check for closed windows.\n'[39m +

                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        This will be called periodically by the runtime.\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +

                    [32m'\n'[39m +

                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        **The state must be effectively immutable!** If any of the\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        other functions in this class might be able to mutate the\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        state, you must {py:obj}`copy.deepcopy` or something\n'[39m +

                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        :::\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        :returns: The immutable state to be {py:obj}`pickle`d.\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        """\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        ...\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +

                    [32m'    _now: datetime = field(init=False)\n'[39m +

                    [32m'        self._now = self.now_getter()\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    @override\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    def before_batch(self) -> None:\n'[39m +

                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    @override\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    def on_item(self, value: Any) -> Tuple[datetime, datetime]:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        return (self._now, self._now)\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    @override\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    def on_notify(self) -> datetime:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        self._now = self.now_getter()\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        return self._now\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    @override\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    def on_eof(self) -> datetime:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        return UTC_MAX\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +

                    [32m'    def to_system_utc(self, timestamp: datetime) -> Optional[datetime]:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        return timestamp\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    @override\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    def snapshot(self) -> None:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        return None\n'[39m +

                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'@dataclass\n'[39m +
                    [32m'class _EventClockState:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    system_time_of_max_event: datetime\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    watermark_base: datetime\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'@dataclass\n'[39m +
                    [32m'class _EventClockLogic(ClockLogic[V, _EventClockState]):\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    now_getter: Callable[[], datetime]\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    timestamp_getter: Callable[[V], datetime]\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    to_system: Callable[[datetime], Optional[datetime]]\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    wait_for_system_duration: timedelta\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    state: _EventClockState = field(\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        default_factory=lambda: _EventClockState(\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            system_time_of_max_event=UTC_MIN,\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            watermark_base=UTC_MIN,\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        )\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    )\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    _system_now: datetime = field(init=False)\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    def __post_init__(self) -> None:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        self._system_now = self.now_getter()\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        if self.state.system_time_of_max_event <= UTC_MIN:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            self.state.system_time_of_max_event = self._system_now\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    @override\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    def before_batch(self) -> None:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        system_now = self.now_getter()\n'[39m +
                    [32m"        # Don't let now go backwards. This can happen due to all\n"[39m +
                    [32m'        # manner of things: NTP synchronization, updating the system\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        # clock, negative leap seconds. This is especially prevalent\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        # on M1 Macs for unclear reasons.\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        if system_now > self._system_now:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            self._system_now = system_now\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        # Even if this happens, the dataflow will continue to process\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        # items, but the watermark will hold steady until the system\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        # clock catches up.\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    @override\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    def on_item(self, value: V) -> Tuple[datetime, datetime]:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        value_event_timestamp = self.timestamp_getter(value)\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        watermark = self.state.watermark_base + (\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            self._system_now - self.state.system_time_of_max_event\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        )\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        try:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            watermark_base = value_event_timestamp - self.wait_for_system_duration\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            if watermark_base > watermark:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                assert watermark_base >= self.state.watermark_base\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                self.state.watermark_base = watermark_base\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                assert self._system_now >= self.state.system_time_of_max_event\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                self.state.system_time_of_max_event = self._system_now\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                return value_event_timestamp, watermark_base\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        except OverflowError:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            # Since the new actual watermark is unrepresentable, we\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            # need to keep the watermark advancing at UTC_MIN + system\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            # time, so that the watermark does not regress.\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            pass\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        return value_event_timestamp, watermark\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    @override\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    def on_notify(self) -> datetime:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        if self.state is None:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            return UTC_MIN\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        else:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            self.before_batch()\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            watermark = self.state.watermark_base + (\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                self._system_now - self.state.system_time_of_max_event\n'[39m +

                    [32m'            return watermark\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    @override\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    def on_eof(self) -> datetime:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        return UTC_MAX\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    @override\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    def to_system_utc(self, timestamp: datetime) -> Optional[datetime]:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        return self.to_system(timestamp)\n'[39m +

                    [32m'    def snapshot(self) -> _EventClockState:\n'[39m +

                    [32m'class FileSource(FixedPartitionedSource[str, int]):\n'[39m +

                    [32m'        {py:obj}`list_parts`.\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        Do not pre-build state about a partition in the\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        constructor. All state must be derived from `resume_state` for\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        recovery to work properly.\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        :arg step_id: The step_id of the output operator.\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        :arg for_part: Which partition to build. Will always be one of\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            the keys returned by {py:obj}`list_parts` on this worker.\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        :arg resume_state: State data containing where in the output\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            stream this partition should be begin writing during this\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            execution.\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +

                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        """\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        ...\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'class StatelessSinkPartition(ABC, Generic[X]):\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    """Output partition that is stateless."""\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    @abstractmethod\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    def write_batch(self, items: List[X]) -> None:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        """Write a batch of output items.\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        Called multiple times whenever new items are seen at this\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        point in the dataflow.\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        :arg items: Items in the dataflow. Non-deterministically\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            batched.\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        """\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        ...\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    def close(self) -> None:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        """Cleanup this partition when the dataflow completes.\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        This is not guaranteed to be called. It will only be called\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        when the dataflow finishes on finite input. It will not be\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        called during an abrupt or abort shutdown.\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        """\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        return\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'class DynamicSink(Sink[X]):\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    """An output sink where all workers write items concurrently.\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    Does not support storing any resume state. Thus these kind of\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    outputs only naively can support at-least-once processing.\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    """\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    @abstractmethod\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    def build(\n'[39m +

                    [32m'    ) -> StatelessSinkPartition[X]:\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        """Build an output partition for a worker.\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        Will be called once on each worker.\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        :arg step_id: The step_id of the output operator.\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        :arg worker_index: Index of this worker.\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        :arg worker_count: Total number of workers.\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        :returns: The built partition.\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        """\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        ...\n'[39m +
                    [32m''[39m,
                  isTruncated: [33mfalse[39m
                }

                  [32m'    """item is (window_id, list_of_records). Returns (window_start, line) or None."""\n'[39m +

                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    window_start = ALIGN_TO + window_id * WINDOW_LENGTH\n'[39m +

                  [32m'    correlations = []\n'[39m +

                  [32m'        for j in range(i + 1, len(sensors)):\n'[39m +

                  [32m'            ys = [by_sensor[b][t] for t in common_ts]\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            mean_x = sum(xs) / n\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            mean_y = sum(ys) / n\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            num = sum((x - mean_x) * (y - mean_y) for x, y in zip(xs, ys))\n'[39m +

                  [32m'            var_y = sum((y - mean_y) ** 2 for y in ys)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            den = math.sqrt(var_x * var_y)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            if den == 0:\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                r_val = None\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            else:\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                r_val = round(num / den, 6)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                if r_val == 0:\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                    # Normalize away any signed-zero artifact from rounding.\n'[39m +
                  [32m'                    r_val = 0.0\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'            correlations.append({"pair": [a, b], "n": n, "r": r_val})\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    if not correlations:\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        return None\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    result = {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        "window_start": window_start.strftime(TS_FORMAT),\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        "window_end": window_end.strftime(TS_FORMAT),\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        "correlations": correlations,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    return (window_start, json.dumps(result))\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'def finalize(acc):\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    lines = [line for _start, line in sorted(acc, key=lambda t: t[0])]\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    return lines\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'flow = Dataflow("sensor_correlations")\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'lines_in = op.input("read_lines", flow, FileSource(str(INPUT_PATH)))\n'[39m +
                  [32m'records = op.map("parse_line", lines_in, parse_line)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'keyed_records = op.key_on("key_on_const", records, lambda _r: "all")\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'clock = EventClock(\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    ts_getter=get_timestamp,\n'[39m +
